  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "The contradiction argument for five colors. The transfer from arbitrary proper colorings to weak measurable colorings is proved for every finite number of colors; the geometric obstruction uses five labels.",
      "figure_id": "fig:proof-route",
      "pdf_page": 5,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:proof-route",
          "exact_quote": "The contradiction argument for five colors. The transfer from\narbitrary proper colorings to weak measurable colorings is proved for\nevery finite number of colors; the geometric obstruction uses five labels.",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n  font=\\small,\n  stage/.style={draw=black!55, rounded corners=2pt, fill=black!3,\n    align=center, text width=2.48cm, minimum height=1.05cm,\n    inner sep=5pt},\n  link/.style={-{Stealth[length=2mm]}, semithick, draw=black!70},\n  node distance=0.48cm]\n  \\node[stage] (proper) {Arbitrary proper\\\\five-coloring};\n  \\node[stage, right=of proper] (law)\n    {Invariant probability\\\\on proper labelings};\n  \\node[stage, right=of law] (factor)\n    {Continuous\\\\translation factor};\n  \\node[stage, right=of factor] (weak)\n    {Weak measurable\\\\five-coloring};\n  \\node[stage] (palettes) at ([yshift=-2.28cm]weak.center)\n    {Angular palettes\\\\of size at most two};\n  \\node[stage] (cycle) at ([yshift=-2.28cm]factor.center)\n    {Common exclusion\\\\continua on a cycle\\\\of length $3$, $4$, or $5$};\n  \\node[stage, text width=2.48cm,\n    font=\\footnotesize, minimum height=1.27cm] (cases)\n    at ([yshift=-2.28cm]law.center)\n    {$3$: Moser graph\\\\$4$: parity obstruction\\\\$5$: six-step words};\n  \\node[stage, fill=black!9] (end) at ([yshift=-2.28cm]proper.center)\n    {Contradiction};\n  \\draw[link] (proper) -- (law);\n  \\draw[link] (law) -- (factor);\n  \\draw[link] (factor) -- (weak);\n  \\draw[link] (weak) -- (palettes);\n  \\draw[link] (palettes) -- (cycle);\n  \\draw[link] (cycle) -- (cases);\n  \\draw[link] (cases) -- (end);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A flow diagram showing eight rectangular boxes connected by arrows in a loop-like sequence from top-left to bottom-left:\n- Box containing \"Arbitrary proper five-coloring\", pointing right via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing \"Invariant probability on proper labelings\", pointing right via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing \"Continuous translation factor\", pointing right via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing \"Weak measurable five-coloring\".\nFrom \"Weak measurable five-coloring\", a vertical arrow points downwards to:\n- Box containing \"Angular palettes of size at most two\", pointing left via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing \"Common exclusion continua on a cycle of length 3, 4, or 5\", pointing left via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing three lines: \"3: Moser graph\", \"4: parity obstruction\", \"5: six-step words\", pointing left via a horizontal arrow to:\n- Box containing \"Contradiction\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The strict inequalities $|z−q|",
      "figure_id": "fig:exclusion-continuum",
      "pdf_page": 43,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:exclusion-continuum",
          "exact_quote": "The strict inequalities $|z-q|<1<|z-x|$, with $x,q\\in K_j$,\nput $z$ in $\\Delta(K_j)$. Connectedness supplies a point $p\\in K_j$\nwith $|z-p|=1$. Both endpoint essential supports are excluded from $\\Delta(K_j)$. The shape is schematic: the pr",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=2.65cm,y=2.65cm,font=\\small,\n  point/.style={circle,fill=black,inner sep=1.45pt}]\n  \\coordinate (X) at (0,0);\n  \\coordinate (Z) at (1.25,0);\n  \\coordinate (Q) at (.65,.45);\n  \\coordinate (P) at (.3,{sqrt(1-.95*.95)});\n  \\coordinate (F) at ({cos(40)},{sin(40)});\n\n  \\fill[black!2] (Z) circle[radius=1];\n  \\draw[black!35,densely dashed] (X) circle[radius=1];\n  \\node[black!60,anchor=south east] at (-.58,.83)\n    {$\\partial B(x,\\rho)$};\n  \\draw[black!65,densely dotted,semithick] (Z) circle[radius=1];\n  \\node[black!65,anchor=north west] at (1.78,-.85)\n    {$\\{y:|y-z|=1\\}$};\n\n  \\draw[black!65] (X) -- node[below=5pt] {$|z-x|>1$} (Z);\n  \\draw[black!65] (Q) -- node[above right=2pt] {$|z-q|<1$} (Z);\n\n  \\draw[blue!55!black,line width=1.45pt,line cap=round]\n    (X) .. controls (.045,.01) and (.025,.13) .. (.085,.14)\n    .. controls (.135,.15) and (.12,.07) .. (.17,.085)\n    .. controls (.245,.105) and (.18,.28) .. (.235,.295)\n    .. controls (.26,.32) and (.28,.30) .. (P)\n    .. controls (.34,.34) and (.35,.48) .. (.405,.475)\n    .. controls (.48,.47) and (.48,.385) .. (.545,.40)\n    .. controls (.59,.41) and (.62,.46) .. (Q)\n    .. controls (.70,.43) and (.66,.575) .. (.715,.59)\n    .. controls (.74,.605) and (.75,.625) .. (F);\n  \\node[blue!55!black,anchor=south] at (.43,.55) {$K_j$};\n\n  \\node[point,label={[label distance=3pt]left:$x$}] at (X) {};\n  \\node[point,label={[label distance=3pt]right:$z$}] at (Z) {};\n  \\node[point,label={[label distance=3pt]below left:$q$}] at (Q) {};\n  \\node[point,label={[label distance=3pt]above left:$p$}] at (P) {};\n  \\node[point,fill=blue!55!black] at (F) {};\n  \\node[align=center] at (.65,-1.31)\n    {$z\\in\\Delta(K_j)$\\quad and\\quad\n      $z\\notin H_{i_j}\\cup H_{i_{j+1}}$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A geometric diagram illustrating the straddling mechanism. On the left, a point $x$ lies on a dashed circular boundary labeled $\\partial B(x, \\rho)$. A continuous blue curve labeled $K_j$ starts at $x$ and contains marked points $p$ and $q$. To the right is a point $z$, surrounded by a dashed circular arc labeled $\\{y : |y - z| = 1\\}$. Straight line segments connect $z$ to points on $K_j$: a segment from $z$ to $x$ labeled with $|z - x| > 1$, a segment from $z$ to $q$ labeled with $|z - q| < 1$, and a segment from $z$ to $p$ marked with a single tick mark indicating unit distance $|z - p| = 1$. Below the diagram is the centered expression $z \\in \\Delta(K_j) \\text{ and } z \\notin H_{i_j} \\cup H_{i_{j+1}}$."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The Moser graph in the placement $z_{g}$. All eleven drawn edges have unit length. The two triangles sharing $z_{A}z_{B}$ force $z_{0}, z_{T}$ to have the same color in any proper three-coloring; the dashed diamond forces $z_{0}, z_{uT}$ to have the same color. The highlighted edge $z_{T}z_{uT}$ contradicts these equalities. The shading gives a cropped view of the open region [eq:source-27], using sampled analytic boundaries; the boundaries and radii zero, one, and two are excluded. Dotted rays mark the sector boundaries at multiples of $π/3$. The rational certificate in Section 8.6 proves strict membership of every vertex in that region, which is restricted almost everywhere to three labels.",

          "source_object": "fig:0003"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=3.8cm,y=3.8cm,font=\\small,\n  vertex/.style={circle,fill=black,inner sep=1.7pt},\n  first/.style={blue!55!black,line width=.9pt},\n  second/.style={black!65,line width=.9pt,densely dashed}]\n  \\begin{scope}\n    \\clip (-1.68,-.24) rectangle (.68,1.93);\n    \\foreach \\sector in {0,60,120,180,240,300} {\n      \\path[fill=teal!12,draw=teal!45!black,line width=.25pt]\n        plot[domain=0:1,samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector+acos(\\r/2)-60)},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector+acos(\\r/2)-60)})\n        -- plot[domain=1:0,samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector-acos(\\r/2)+60)},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector-acos(\\r/2)+60)}) -- cycle;\n      \\path[fill=teal!12,draw=teal!45!black,line width=.25pt]\n        plot[domain=1:sqrt(3),samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector+60-acos(\\r/2))},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector+60-acos(\\r/2))})\n        -- plot[domain=sqrt(3):2,samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector+acos(\\r/2))},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector+acos(\\r/2))})\n        -- plot[domain=2:sqrt(3),samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector-acos(\\r/2))},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector-acos(\\r/2))})\n        -- plot[domain=sqrt(3):1,samples=101,variable=\\r]\n          ({\\r*cos(\\sector-60+acos(\\r/2))},\n           {\\r*sin(\\sector-60+acos(\\r/2))}) -- cycle;\n    }\n    \\foreach \\sector in {0,60,120,180,240,300}\n      \\draw[black!25,densely dotted,line width=.35pt]\n        (0,0) -- (\\sector:2);\n    \\draw[fill=white,draw=black!45,line width=.3pt]\n      (0,0) circle[radius=.012];\n  \\end{scope}\n  \\foreach \\name/\\gx/\\gy in {\n    O/0/0,\n    A/{sqrt(3)/2}/{1/2},\n    B/{sqrt(3)/2}/{-1/2},\n    T/{sqrt(3)}/0,\n    UA/{(5*sqrt(3)-sqrt(11))/12}/{(sqrt(33)+5)/12},\n    UB/{(5*sqrt(3)+sqrt(11))/12}/{(sqrt(33)-5)/12},\n    UT/{5*sqrt(3)/6}/{sqrt(33)/6}}\n  {\n    \\coordinate (\\name) at\n      ({(35*(\\gx-290/250)-12*(\\gy+149/250))/37},\n       {(12*(\\gx-290/250)+35*(\\gy+149/250))/37});\n  }\n  \\draw[first] (O) -- (A) -- (T) -- (B) -- (O);\n  \\draw[first] (A) -- (B);\n  \\draw[second] (O) -- (UA) -- (UT) -- (UB) -- (O);\n  \\draw[second] (UA) -- (UB);\n  \\draw[orange!70!black,line width=1.5pt] (T) --\n    node[midway,right=5pt,text=black] {$1$} (UT);\n\n  \\node[vertex,label=left:$z_0$] at (O) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=above:$z_A$] at (A) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=below:$z_B$] at (B) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=right:$z_T$] at (T) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=left:$z_{uA}$] at (UA) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=below right:$z_{uB}$] at (UB) {};\n  \\node[vertex,label=above:$z_{uT}$] at (UT) {};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A geometric embedding of the Moser spindle graph $z_g$ over a light-blue shaded planar region with sampled analytic boundaries and dotted rays emanating at sector angles (multiples of $\\pi/3$).\nSeven vertices are labeled:\n- $z_0$ (bottom-left)\n- $z_A$ (center-left)\n- $z_B$ (bottom-center)\n- $z_T$ (right)\n- $z_{uA}$ (upper-left)\n- $z_{uB}$ (center)\n- $z_{uT}$ (top-center)\nThe edges comprise:\n- Five solid black edges forming two equilateral triangles sharing the edge between $z_A$ and $z_B$, with outer vertices $z_0$ and $z_T$ (edges $z_0 z_A$, $z_0 z_B$, $z_A z_B$, $z_A z_T$, $z_B z_T$).\n- Five dashed black edges forming two equilateral triangles sharing the edge between $z_{uA}$ and $z_{uB}$, with outer vertices $z_0$ and $z_{uT}$ (edges $z_0 z_{uA}$, $z_0 z_{uB}$, $z_{uA} z_{uB}$, $z_{uA} z_{uT}$, $z_{uB} z_{uT}$).\n- A single highlighted solid edge connecting $z_T$ and $z_{uT}$, labeled with unit length \"1\"."
    }
